#### `regime.engine` - `regime/engine.py`

**Role:** indicator-engine | **Lines:** 359 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Computes a three-axis index regime for one cycle: direction (BULL/BEAR/SIDEWAYS from EMA position, EMA cross, slope, swing structure and ADX), volatility (current ATR vs the mean true range) and day-type (TREND/RANGE/UNDETERMINED from intraday range vs ATR, directionality, position in range and session progress), each with an ordinal confidence, plus a positive-confirmation-only extreme flag. compute() is contractually fail-safe: every failure path returns unknown_state with a neutral multiplier rather than raising.

- **Imported by (1):** `regime`
- **Imports locally (3):** `core.candle_math`, `regime.models`, `sr_detector.pivots`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `_attr` (L53) - getattr with a default — every config read goes through it | `MarketRegimeEngine` (L57) - the engine; compute() never raises | `MarketRegimeEngine.__init__` (L60) - reads all thresholds off config and derives _min_daily_candles = max(ema_slow+1, 2*adx_period+1) (92) | `enabled` (L95) - property exposing the config `enabled` flag (read by the runner's start()) | `compute` (L100) - fetches daily+intraday index candles, runs the three axes through _safe_axis, and returns RegimeState(STATUS_OK) or unknown_state | `_direction` (L142) - votes bull vs bear across four sub-signals and grades confidence HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/TRANSITION | `_swing_structure` (L190) - higher-highs+higher-lows vs lower-highs+lower-lows from find_swing_pivots | `_volatility` (L205) - ATR/baseline-TR ratio against vol_high_ratio / vol_low_ratio with boundary-aware confidence | `_day_type` (L233) - TREND_DAY needs wide range + one-way + close at an extreme; RANGE_DAY needs narrow + low directionality + mid position | `_extreme_flag` (L283) - TRUE only on a confirmed halt; a missing feed logs CRITICAL and returns False | `_is_confirmed_halt` (L302) - static interpreter of bool/str/dict exchange-status payloads (HALT/HALTED/CIRCUIT/CLOSED/SUSPENDED) | `_fetch` (L321) - fetcher.fetch_by_token(token, interval, lookback_days); None + warning on any failure
- **Inputs:** config attributes read in __init__ (77-91); index candles from fetcher.fetch_by_token (323) — daily and intraday, using .open/.high/.low/.close; now from the injected now_fn or the compute(now=...) argument (105); market_windows.market_open / market_close (332-333); optional exchange_status_fn() payload (295)
- **Outputs:** RegimeState(STATUS_OK, direction, volatility, day_type, extreme_flag, preference_multiplier=direction.multiplier, ts) (127-135); unknown_state(...) on insufficient candles (115) or any exception (138); AxisResult objects with an `evidence` dict per axis (181, 227, 275)
- **Side effects:** log records only, all through _safe_log (110, 137, 288, 297, 325, 350) — including a CRITICAL every cycle when the exchange-status feed is absent (288); no DB, no file, no broker order; the only outbound call is the injected fetcher

- **Config keys read:** `enabled` (L77), `index_token` (L78), `index_symbol` (L79), `daily_lookback_days` (L80), `intraday_interval` (L81), `intraday_lookback_days` (L82), `ema_fast` (L83), `ema_slow` (L84), `slope_lookback` (L85), `adx_period` (L86), `adx_trend_threshold` (L87), `atr_period` (L88), `vol_high_ratio` (L89), `vol_low_ratio` (L90)
- **Paper vs live:** none visible — the docstring states "Pure w.r.t. injected collaborators → paper==live by construction (no mode branch)" (18-19) and no mode flag appears in the file.

#### `regime.runner` - `regime/runner.py`

**Role:** shadow-runner | **Lines:** 118 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The default-off shadow runner around MarketRegimeEngine: a daemon thread that, during market hours, calls engine.compute() once per interval, logs the one-line verdict, keeps it in memory as `latest`, and atomically persists the latest state to a JSON file for later prior_day_regime use. It gates nothing (docstring line 6-7) and is written so no cycle error can kill the thread.

- **Imported by (2):** `regime`, `tests.unit.test_market_regime`

- **Inputs:** constructor: engine, logger, now_fn, market_windows, interval_sec, persist_path (20-29); engine.compute() return value (64); market_windows.is_market_open(now) (81); read_persisted_regime(persist_path) — a JSON file path (111)
- **Outputs:** RegimeState from run_once (75) and via the `latest` property (41); a JSON file at persist_path containing {"date": <ts[:10]>, "regime": state.to_dict()} (93-98); one INFO log line per cycle with status, all three axes, preference and extreme flag (66-73)

#### `sr_detector.confluence` - `sr_detector/confluence.py`

**Role:** scoring (pure) | **Lines:** 211 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pure scoring stage of the S&R detector, used by sr_detector/zone_builder.py and sr_detector/zone_warmer.py. It merges same-kind zones across timeframes by centre proximity into one band (:80) and scores each merged band as a weighted sum of independent votes — swing touches, number of timeframes, a volume node, a prior-day level, a round number, and recency — then buckets the total into HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW while keeping the per-vote evidence breakdown for audit (:149-159).

- **Imported by (3):** `sr_detector.zone_builder`, `sr_detector.zone_warmer`, `tests.unit.test_sr_detector_units`
- **Imports locally (1):** `sr_detector.models`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `ConfluenceContext` (L37) - Frozen inputs overlaid onto raw zones: prior_day_levels, volume_nodes, now, recency_window_days (default 90). | `ScoringParams` (L46) - Frozen weights and thresholds: w_swing/w_volume/w_multi_tf/w_prior_day/w_round/w_recency, t_high, t_med, touch_cap, merge_pct, band_buffer_pct, recency_min_factor. | `score_zones` (L62) - The only public entry: merges and scores RESISTANCE then SUPPORT separately and returns a flat list of ScoredZone. | `_merge_across_tfs` (L80) - Greedy clustering of same-kind zones from every timeframe by distance to the running group centre, tolerance = centre * merge_pct/100. | `_score_group` (L104) - Builds the merged band (min band_low / max band_high), sums the weighted votes into score, and constructs the ScoredZone with evidence. | `_bucket` (L162) - HIGH requires score>=t_high AND >=2 evidence entries AND >=2 timeframes; MEDIUM requires score>=t_med; else LOW. | `_level_in_band` (L170) - True when any supplied level falls inside the band widened by band_buffer_pct. | `_has_round_number` (L180) - True when a multiple of the price magnitude or half-magnitude (10^floor(log10(centre))) falls inside the band. | `_recency_factor` (L195) - Linear decay 1 - age_days/recency_window_days, clamped to [recency_min_factor, 1.0], with a naive/aware tz-safety normalisation (:204-206).
- **Inputs:** zones_by_tf: {timeframe: [Zone]}, a ConfluenceContext and a ScoringParams (:62-66)
- **Outputs:** List[ScoredZone] with band_low/band_high, kind, score, confidence, touches, timeframes, evidence tuple and last_touch_ts (:149-159)
- **Side effects:** None — pure functions, no I/O, no global mutation
- **Unknowns:** Who supplies ConfluenceContext.volume_nodes / prior_day_levels and whether they are ever non-empty at runtime (built in zone_builder / zone_warmer, not here).; Which config keys map onto ScoringParams fields — the params object is constructed elsewhere.; Whether the greedy, order-dependent merge at :95-100 (comparison against the running group mean) is intended to allow a group to drift wider than merge_pct end-to-end.

#### `sr_detector.detector` - `sr_detector/detector.py`

**Role:** shadow observer / worker | **Lines:** 329 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The non-gating shadow observer: signal_processor hands it an already-PLACED Candidate, observe() enqueues it and returns immediately (:131-140), and one serialized daemon worker (:114) does the slow work — three historical fetches, zone building, confluence scoring, flag/retest computation — then writes one evidence row via store.insert_sr_detector_result (:295). Every failure path is swallowed so nothing reaches the order pipeline (:139, :154, :299). A default-OFF gate (intraday_anchors_enabled, :84) adds Layer-A VWAP/ORB anchors plus swing labelling to the persisted evidence JSON (:264-268).

- **Imported by (1):** `sr_detector`
- **Imports locally (4):** `core.effect_telemetry`, `sr_detector.flags`, `sr_detector.models`, `sr_detector.zone_builder`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `SRDetector` (L54) - The observer; __init__ resolves the effect-telemetry handle (:74), the zone/scoring/flag params (:93-95) and a bounded queue (:100). | `SRDetector.enabled` (L107) - Property exposing the config `enabled` flag captured at construction. | `SRDetector.start` (L110) - Starts the single daemon worker thread named "sr-detector" unless disabled or already started. | `SRDetector.stop` (L118) - Sets the stop event, pushes a sentinel, joins the worker with a timeout and clears the handle. | `SRDetector.observe` (L131) - The non-gating seam: put_nowait the candidate; a full queue logs a warning and drops it, and no exception can escape. | `SRDetector._run` (L144) - Worker loop: get with 0.25 s timeout, skip the sentinel, process, and log any error per item. | `SRDetector.process_candidate` (L161) - Public synchronous analyse-then-write, so tests can drive the pipeline deterministically. | `SRDetector.analyze` (L167) - fetch_timeframes → scored_zones_from_candles → breakout context → compute_flags_and_retest, returning an SRAnalysis (FETCH_FAILED early-return at :170). | `SRDetector._build_anchors` (L209) - Layer-A anchors: prior-day/round numbers always, plus a today-only fine-interval fetch for VWAP/ORB when session bounds were injected; any failure yields None fields. | `SRDetector._breakout_context` (L237) - Builds last close/high/low/volume and the trailing average volume from 30minute, else 60minute, else day candles. | `SRDetector._evidence` (L252) - Caps and serialises resistance/support zones to max_zones_logged each. | `SRDetector._write` (L259) - Assembles the 24-field shadow row, inserts it through the store, increments the effect counter, and never raises.

Detector-side fetch orchestration used by sr_detector's factory, main.py and tests: resolve a symbol to an instrument_token through the injected instrument cache (:105), fetch one window per timeframe through the injected rate-limited fetch_fn (:125), convert rows to Candle objects (:126), cache them in-process with a TTL (:138-151), and fail safe so that a token miss, an exception or an empty result yields no candles instead of propagating (:110, :130).

- **Inputs:** Constructor: fetch_fn(token, from_dt, to_dt, interval), instrument_cache, lookback_days, logger, now_fn, cache_ttl_sec (:33-42); fetch_timeframes(symbol, intervals) / fetch_interval(symbol, interval, lookback_days) / fetch_by_token(token, interval, lookback_days, cache_key)
- **Outputs:** {interval: [Candle]} from fetch_timeframes (:63-68); Optional[List[Candle]] from fetch_interval and fetch_by_token (:83, :101)
- **Side effects:** Calls the injected broker fetch closure (:125); Populates and mutates the in-process cache dict under a lock (:151); Warning logs on token-lookup and fetch failure (:111, :131-135); No DB writes, no file writes
- **External calls:** Broker historical_data, via the injected fetch_fn (:125); the docstring states main.py builds it as a closure over the market-data kite handle and the historical rate limiter (:11-15); instrument_cache.get_by_symbol (:107)

#### `sr_detector.flags` - `sr_detector/flags.py`

**Role:** flagging (pure) | **Lines:** 186 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pure flag/proposal stage used by sr_detector/detector.py and zone_builder-adjacent tests. Given a placed Candidate and its confluence-scored zones, it picks the nearest resistance and support (:65-66), computes signed distances in percent (:68-69), and emits an ordered, de-duplicated tuple of structural flags plus a retest PROPOSAL (entry at the band edge, SL a configured percent beyond it) for the long and short mirrors.

- **Imported by (3):** `sr_detector.detector`, `sr_detector.zone_builder`, `tests.unit.test_sr_detector_units`
- **Imports locally (1):** `sr_detector.models`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `BreakoutContext` (L28) - Frozen recent price action: last close/high/low/volume and the trailing average volume. | `FlagParams` (L38) - Frozen knobs: entry_proximity_pct, volume_surge_mult, weak_breakout_frac, retest_sl_buffer_pct. | `FlagResult` (L46) - Frozen output: nearest zones, the two distance percentages, the flag tuple and the RetestProposal. | `compute_flags_and_retest` (L55) - The only public entry: no zones → NO_CLEAR_STRUCTURE; long → BUYING_INTO_RESISTANCE (HIGH confidence) else LOW_CONFIDENCE_STRUCTURE; short mirrors; then the breakout/volume flags and an order-preserving de-dupe. | `_nearest` (L114) - Nearest zone by centre distance, preferring zones whose band_high >= entry (resistance) or band_low <= entry (support), falling back to all zones. | `_broken_zone` (L125) - The zone a breakout is passing through: for a long the highest resistance with band_high <= entry, for a short the lowest support with band_low >= entry. | `_is_into_zone` (L138) - Long: entry inside the band or within centre*proximity_pct below it, but not above band_high; short mirrors. | `_breakout_flags` (L149) - Appends WEAK_BREAKOUT when the close barely clears the band or pokes through and closes back inside, and NO_VOLUME_CONFIRMATION when a breakout's volume is below volume_surge_mult × average. | `_retest_for_long` (L178) - Proposal: wait, enter at band_high, SL band_low reduced by retest_sl_buffer_pct. | `_retest_for_short` (L184) - Mirror proposal: enter at band_low, SL band_high raised by retest_sl_buffer_pct.
- **Inputs:** candidate (Candidate), scored_zones (List[ScoredZone]), breakout (Optional[BreakoutContext]), params (FlagParams) (:55-60)
- **Outputs:** FlagResult with nearest_resistance / nearest_support, dist_to_resistance_pct / dist_to_support_pct, an ordered flag tuple and a RetestProposal (:107)
- **Side effects:** None — pure functions; the only mutation is the local `flags` list
- **Unknowns:** Which config keys populate FlagParams (built outside this module).; Whether a WEAK_BREAKOUT/NO_VOLUME_CONFIRMATION flag is ever consumed downstream — this file only returns them.

#### `sr_detector.pivots` - `sr_detector/pivots.py`

**Role:** structure detection (pure) | **Lines:** 55 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Single-timeframe swing-pivot detection, used by sr_detector/zone_builder.py, regime/engine.py and the v3_chain runners. A bar at index i is a HIGH pivot when its high is >= every high in the window [i-left, i+right] and strictly greater than at least one neighbour in that window (so a flat plateau does not mark every bar); LOW pivots are the mirror on lows.

- **Imported by (5):** `regime.engine`, `sr_detector.zone_builder`, `tests.unit.test_sr_detector_units`, `v3_chain.pb01_runner`, `v3_chain.runner`

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `find_swing_pivots` (L24) - Returns the HIGH and LOW Pivot objects for a chronological candle list given the left/right window sizes; the first `left` and last `right` bars are skipped (:42).
- **Inputs:** candles: List[Candle] (chronological), left: int, right: int (:24)
- **Outputs:** List[Pivot] in bar order, each carrying price, ts, kind and bar_index (:49, :53)
- **Side effects:** None — pure function
- **Unknowns:** Where left/right come from per timeframe (supplied by the callers, e.g. zone_builder).

#### `sr_detector.retest_confirm` - `sr_detector/retest_confirm.py`

**Role:** state machine (pure) | **Lines:** 137 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The pure WAIT_FOR_RETEST confirmation state machine used by main.py, orders/structure_exit_manager.py and screening/retest_monitor.py. evaluate() folds the 1-minute candles since divert over the states WAIT_BREAKOUT → WAIT_RETEST → WAIT_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED, direction-relative (LONG breaks above band_high, SHORT below band_low), and returns REJECT on timeout or on a close more than max_away_pct the wrong way past the band. Because the result is a pure function of (zone, direction, candles, elapsed), the monitor can recompute it every poll without mis-advancing.

- **Imported by (5):** `main`, `orders.structure_exit_manager`, `screening.retest_monitor`, `tests.unit.test_sr_v2_monitor`, `tests.unit.test_sr_v2_retest_confirm`
- **Imports locally (0):** none
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `WAIT_BREAKOUT` (L38) - The five state strings, shared by both trade directions. | `IN_PROGRESS_STATES` (L45) - The three non-terminal states, i.e. the ones persisted in retest_state.state. | `_is_long` (L50) - Direction test: LONG or BUY (case/space tolerant). | `RetestParams` (L55) - timeout_sec, max_away_pct, confirm_strong_close_frac and an optional breakout_margin_pct (default 0). | `RetestResult` (L63) - state + reason, with confirmed (:68), rejected (:72) and terminal (:76) convenience properties. | `evaluate` (L80) - Keyword-only entry: timeout check first, then builds the directional break/reject predicates and folds the candles to a state. | `_strong_close` (L130) - Directional strong close — LONG wants the close in the upper frac of the 1m range, SHORT the lower frac.
- **Inputs:** band_low, band_high, candles (1m Candle-likes with .open/.high/.low/.close), elapsed_sec, params (RetestParams), direction (:80-88)
- **Outputs:** RetestResult(state, reason) — REJECT/"timeout" (:90), REJECT/fail_reason (:114), CONFIRMED/confirm_reason (:125), or the in-progress state with reason "in_progress" (:127)
- **Side effects:** None — pure function; no I/O and no state kept between calls
- **Unknowns:** Which candles the caller passes (whether the list is trimmed to post-divert bars) and how elapsed_sec is measured — both decided by screening/retest_monitor.py.; Which config keys populate RetestParams, and whether the persisted retest_state row keeps the reason string.

#### `sr_detector.zone_cache` - `sr_detector/zone_cache.py`

**Role:** in-process TTL cache | **Lines:** 76 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

An in-memory, RLock-guarded, per-symbol store of the latest confluence-scored zones, split into resistance and support tuples with the warm timestamp (ZoneSet, line 23). Its importers are main.py and sr_detector/zone_warmer.py.

#### `sr_detector.zone_warmer` - `sr_detector/zone_warmer.py`

**Role:** background worker / cache warmer | **Lines:** 161 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

A daemon-thread worker that keeps the ZoneCache warm so the pre-placement divert check never fetches: on a queued symbol it fetches the structure timeframes through the injected OhlcFetcher, builds scored zones with the shared zone_builder, and stores resistance+support in the cache (warm, line 129). When the queue is idle it rescans the cache and re-enqueues symbols nearing TTL expiry (lines 106-108, 121-126). Imported by main.py; the file states it never raises into the caller and never does the 1-minute fetch.

- **Imported by (2):** `main`, `tests.unit.test_sr_v2_zone_cache`
- **Imports locally (3):** `sr_detector.confluence`, `sr_detector.zone_builder`, `sr_detector.zone_cache`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `ZoneWarmer` (L30) - the worker class | `ZoneWarmer.__init__` (L31) - takes fetcher, cache, knobs, scoring, logger, now_fn and the queue/margin/idle knobs; builds a bounded Queue and the pending set | `ZoneWarmer.start` (L63) - spawns the daemon thread 'sr-zone-warmer' unless disabled or already started | `ZoneWarmer.stop` (L71) - sets the stop event, pushes the sentinel, joins with a timeout | `ZoneWarmer.enqueue` (L84) - deduped, non-raising enqueue of a symbol; logs and drops on a full queue | `ZoneWarmer._run` (L103) - the worker loop: get with timeout, on Empty do the rewarm scan, else warm the symbol | `ZoneWarmer._enqueue_rewarms` (L121) - re-enqueues every symbol the cache reports as due | `ZoneWarmer.warm` (L129) - public synchronous fetch -> build -> cache put; returns True only when zones were cached | `ZoneWarmer._safe_now` (L146) - now_fn() or None on any exception | `ZoneWarmer._safe_log` (L152) - level-dispatched logging that swallows everything, including a None logger | `_SENTINEL` (L161) - module-level stop marker pushed onto the queue
- **Inputs:** fetcher, cache, knobs, scoring, logger, now_fn, rewarm_margin_sec=300.0, idle_sleep_sec=2.0, max_queue=512, enabled=True (lines 33-43); symbols via enqueue (line 84) or a direct warm call (line 129)
- **Outputs:** bool from warm (lines 135, 142); zones written into the injected ZoneCache (line 139); log lines 'zone_warmer: started', the queue-full warning, the fetch-miss info and 'zone_warmer: warmed %s (res=%d sup=%d)' (lines 69, 97, 134, 140-141) -- these are output strings, not code paths
- **Side effects:** starts a daemon thread (lines 67-68); calls the fetcher, which per the docstring performs the rate-limited historical OHLC fetch (line 132); mutates the shared ZoneCache (line 139); mutates internal queue and pending-set state (lines 55-56, 92-96, 114); emits logger calls through _safe_log
- **External calls:** fetcher.fetch_timeframes(symbol, intervals) -- historical OHLC fetch, line 132
- **Unknowns:** the fetcher's actual class and whether it really paces through rate_limiter.acquire('historical') (asserted only in the docstring, line 8); what value of `enabled` production passes and who calls start()/stop(); whether any caller invokes enqueue on signal arrival as the docstring prescribes

#### `sr_shadow` - `sr_shadow/__init__.py`

**Role:** package facade / scope declaration | **Lines:** 21 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The package facade and the contract statement for the S&R SHADOW v1.3 log-only path: it re-exports CONTRACT_VERSION / SCHEMA_VERSION from params and SrShadowService / build_sr_shadow from runner (lines 18-19), and main.py imports build_sr_shadow from this package (main.py:3530). #### `sr_shadow.calendar` - `sr_shadow/calendar.py`

**Role:** calendar adapter | **Lines:** 130 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The trading-calendar adapter for the shadow path: it loads every config/nse_holidays_<year>.yaml it finds, validates each with core.config_loader.NseHolidaysConfig, and delegates session semantics to core.market_windows.MarketWindows.is_trading_holiday (lines 43-66, 81). Its deliberate design point is non-adjudication: a date in a year with no holiday file returns None from is_expected_session (lines 79-80) so the caller never counts it as an expected session. Used by sr_shadow/bars.py, pipeline.py and runner.py.

- **Inputs:** holidays_by_year mapping and an optional special_sessions mapping (lines 28-30); a config directory path (line 45); dates and a count n (lines 77, 83, 94)

#### `sr_shadow.decision` - `sr_shadow/decision.py`

**Role:** decision rules + observation computation | **Lines:** 432 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Implements the section-5 decision path for ONE validity variant (decide_variant, line 51) -- entry-inside-zone check, both-sides structure requirement, locking the nearest support and resistance, the stop invariant, and target reachability -- and separately computes the section-7 observations for that variant (observe_variant, line 290), which include the ATR-derived diagnostics the header says never feed back into the decision. Called by sr_shadow/pipeline.py; the header asserts the locked zone is never re-selected, the stop never moved and the target never widened.

- **Imported by (2):** `sr_shadow.pipeline`, `tests.unit.test_sr_shadow_decision`

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `VariantResult` (L32) - the per-variant outcome: decision, reject code, diagnostics, locked zones, candidates, stop calc, risk, target, invariant detail | `decide_variant` (L51) - runs 5.3-5.10 and sets the reject code to the FIRST failing step, the rest becoming diagnostic flags | `STATIC_UNDEFINED_VARIANT_FIELDS` (L170) - the 14 field names the contract names but gives no formula for -- always reported undefined | `stop_distance_band` (L188) - maps a stop/ATR ratio to one of the six labelled bands | `_div` (L208) - None-safe division guarding a zero denominator | `_nearest_source` (L214) - nearest preliminary zone of a given timeframe among the candidates' sources, tie-broken by zone_id | `_single_timeframe_decision` (L231) - re-runs the decision using only one timeframe's nearest zones, for timeframe_changed_decision_flag | `_zone_fields` (L252) - flattens one FinalZone into ~30 logged fields including JSON-encoded source arrays and pivot ages | `observe_variant` (L290) - builds the whole observation dict for a variant plus a '__undefined__' list of NULL-with-reason field names | `distance_to_nearest_edge` (L425) - minimum |entry - nearest edge| across all valid final zones
- **Inputs:** variant name, final zones, a {final_id: bool} validity map, entry price, direction, strategy_rr, tick_size, slabs, history_meets_split and an optional callback (lines 52-61); for observations additionally atr14, daily_range and the decision date (lines 291-299)
- **Outputs:** VariantResult (line 154); a Dict[str, object] of observations keyed without the variant prefix, including '__undefined__' (lines 304-422); Optional[float] nearest-edge distance (line 432)
- **Side effects:** invokes the caller-supplied on_invariant_violation callback when the stop invariant fails (line 128) -- whatever that callback does is the only outward effect; otherwise pure
- **Unknowns:** the FinalZone/PrelimZone construction and the validity rules (sr_shadow/zones.py) -- this file only consumes them; how keys absent from the observation dict become NULL columns in the stored row; whether any caller supplies on_invariant_violation, and what it does

Assembles ONE flat decision row from an immutable screener-pass snapshot plus raw daily candles: it prepares completed daily/weekly bars, ATR(14) and coverage, adjudicates DATA_UNAVAILABLE, rebuilds the structure map from scratch, then runs decide_variant + observe_variant once per validity variant and reconciles the two into shadow_decision (lines 46-151). #### `sr_shadow.pricing` - `sr_shadow/pricing.py`

**Role:** stop/buffer arithmetic | **Lines:** 131 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The Decimal-exact price arithmetic for the shadow stop: the section-6.5 slab step function (pick_slab, line 36), the 10-paise away-from-level rounding with tick-lattice enforcement (round_stop, line 58), the baseline structural stop (compute_stop, line 84), the A7 invariant assertion (line 104), and the adjacent-slab diagnostic (line 111). The header states the buffer is a percentage of the locked zone's far edge, selected by the slab of the ENTRY price, and that it shares no code path, config key or value with the entry-slippage allowance. Used by sr_shadow/decision.py.

- **Imported by (2):** `sr_shadow.decision`, `tests.unit.test_sr_shadow_pricing`
- **Imports locally (2):** `sr_shadow`, `sr_shadow.params`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `dec` (L20) - Decimal from the shortest float repr, so a price is exact as written | `SlabPick` (L28) - the chosen band: index, pct, lower/upper edges, exact_boundary flag | `pick_slab` (L36) - step function over the slabs; the last bounded band is closed at its upper edge, then an unbounded top band | `round_stop` (L58) - rounds to 0.10 away from the level (FLOOR for LONG, CEILING for SHORT), then onto the tick lattice, moving further away if needed | `StopCalc` (L72) - the full stop record: far edge, slab source price, reference price, pct, rupees, candidate, final stop, slab | `compute_stop` (L84) - far edge minus/plus buffer_pct x far_edge, then round_stop | `stop_invariant_holds` (L104) - LONG stop strictly below, SHORT stop strictly above, the far edge | `adjacent_slab_pct` (L111) - the pct the nearer neighbouring slab would have given; None when the neighbours are equidistant
- **Inputs:** a price and a slab sequence (line 36); a candidate Decimal, direction and tick (line 58); direction, far_edge, entry, tick_size, slabs (lines 84-85)
- **Outputs:** SlabPick (lines 51, 55); a rounded Decimal stop (lines 67-68); StopCalc (line 96); bool invariant result (line 108); Optional[Decimal] adjacent slab pct (lines 124-131)
- **Side effects:** none -- pure Decimal arithmetic; raises ValueError on a slab table with no unbounded top band (line 54) and on a non-positive tick (line 63)
- **Unknowns:** whether any caller can pass a slab table whose unbounded band is not last, or more than one unbounded band (only DEFAULT_SLABS, params.py:140, is visible); the instrument tick sizes actually supplied as tick_size; whether the Decimal exactness survives the float conversion at the row-write boundary (e.g. float(sc.buffer_pct) in decision.py:365)

The log-only S&R shadow runtime. capture() writes one screener-pass snapshot to the durable spool synchronously before returning to the signal path (127-143); one daemon thread then drains PENDING spool rows, computes the decision row via sr_shadow.pipeline and writes it (161-211). build_sr_shadow() (225) is the wiring entry point used by main.py; DailyHistorySource (45) is an LRU cache of RAW daily candles over an injected rate-limited broker fetch closure (77).

#### `sr_shadow.store` - `sr_shadow/store.py`

**Role:** persistence | **Lines:** 253 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The dedicated SQLite store for the shadow dataset: data_store/sr_shadow/sr_shadow.db with four tables (meta, spool, shadow_rows, evaluation_runs) and its own schema version, deliberately outside trading_system.db's migration chain. Every write is one transaction and refuses to overwrite: a duplicate spool snapshot or decision row is rejected, and the evaluator can only fill rows whose evaluated_at IS NULL.

- **Imported by (4):** `scripts.sr_shadow_evaluate`, `sr_shadow.runner`, `tests.unit.test_sr_shadow_evaluate_script`, `tests.unit.test_sr_shadow_pipeline_runner`

#### `sr_shadow.zones` - `sr_shadow/zones.py`

**Role:** pure computation (structure detection) | **Lines:** 457 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pure, deterministic construction of the per-signal S&R structure map: swing pivots on 1D and 1W bars, price clustering into preliminary zones with geometry, per-zone flip state from completed daily closes, opposite-side conflict flags, a sorted-sweep same-side merge, touch-episode counting, and the two validity variants (strict and recency). build_structure_map (444) is the single entry point used by sr_shadow/pipeline.py:110; nothing is cached and the map is rebuilt per signal.

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`

#### `allocation.portfolio_allocator` - `allocation/portfolio_allocator.py`

**Role:** admission-allocator | **Lines:** 336 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The V3 03.05 portfolio allocator: on its own daemon thread it waits for each wall-clock candle boundary plus a drain tail, drains the window buffer, and then either (shadow) computes and logs/persists three regret metrics comparing ranked vs arrival-order admission against one start-of-window capital snapshot, or (enforce) ranks the batch and admits greedily under one portfolio_lock via the injected signal_processor.admit_prepared callback (docstring lines 4-19). #### `allocation.window_buffer` - `allocation/window_buffer.py`

A thread-safe candidate buffer between the N signal-processor worker threads that append (fire-and-forget) and the single allocator admission worker that drains once per window (docstring lines 4-6). It deliberately uses its own small lock rather than the portfolio_lock so appending adds no contention to the reservation path (line 5).

- **Inputs:** append(candidate: ScoredCandidate) — line 23
- **Outputs:** drain() -> List[ScoredCandidate] (line 27); size() -> int (line 34)
- **Side effects:** mutates its own in-memory list under a threading.Lock (lines 20-21, 25, 30-31)

#### `capital.drift_handler` - `capital/drift_handler.py`

**Role:** drift-escalation-policy | **Lines:** 260 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Event-bus subscriber that receives CapitalDriftDetected events and maps the absolute rupee delta onto a four-tier ladder (NOISE / LOG_ONLY / SOFT / HARD), then calls KillSwitch.soft_kill or hard_kill for the escalating tiers. Only events whose source_module is in _ESCALATING_SOURCES (fund_manager, fund_manager_self_check, fund_manager_bucket_overflow, lines 66-70) can escalate; everything else is logged at INFO and ignored. #### `capital.fund_manager` - `capital/fund_manager.py`

**Role:** capital-ledger / single source of truth | **Lines:** 2,583 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Single in-process ledger of capital for this trading system: it holds `_total` plus a per-bucket (intraday/positional) split of available/reserved/used, and every capital mutation in the system goes through its reserve/release/commit_to_used/release_used methods (lines 521, 637, 894, 1204). Each mutation writes one row to the `fm_ledger` table BEFORE touching in-memory state (write-ahead, `_write_ledger` line 2520), then re-checks the identity available+reserved+used == total (`_check_invariant` line 2391) and, on a break, fires `kill_switch.hard_kill` from outside the lock (line 2495). It is consumed by capital.position_sizer, capital.risk_engine, main, orders.eod_squareoff, orders.order_placer, orders.order_reconciler and signals.signal_processor, which also borrow its RLock via `portfolio_lock` (line 426) so approve+reserve is one critical section.

#### `capital.invariant` - `capital/invariant.py`

**Role:** invariant-assertion | **Lines:** 251 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pure, stateless assertion module for the G3 capital invariant: margin_available + margin_reserved + margin_used == cash_floor + min(0, realized_pnl_today), both sides rounded to 2 dp before the tolerance compare (185-189). Exports three helper computations plus the single assertion entry point; raises CapitalInvariantViolation with a 12-field context on breach. Consumed by capital.fund_manager and three test modules.

- **Imported by (4):** `capital.fund_manager`, `tests.crash_test.ct_day3_isolated_batch2`, `tests.unit.test_invariant`, `tests.unit.test_mc3_per_bucket_invariant`
- **Imports locally (1):** `core.exceptions`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `compute_lhs` (L56) - returns margin_available + margin_reserved + margin_used | `compute_rhs` (L75) - returns cash_floor + min(0.0, realized_pnl_today) - profits ignored until T+1 | `compute_tradable_balance` (L92) - alias that delegates to compute_rhs (line 106) | `assert_capital_invariant` (L113) - runs four non-negative guards then the rounded lhs/rhs comparison; raises on breach, returns None otherwise | `_guard_non_negative` (L212) - raises CapitalInvariantViolation named NEGATIVE_<FIELD> when value < -tolerance, deliberately without rounding the value | `_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE` (L49) - 0.01 (one paise), the default tolerance argument
- **Inputs:** margin_available, margin_reserved, margin_used, cash_floor, realized_pnl_today (floats, 114-118); context-only strings bucket, mutation_type, reservation_id (119-121); tolerance override (line 122)
- **Outputs:** None on success (line 147); raises core.exceptions.CapitalInvariantViolation carrying bucket, mutation_type, reservation_id, the five balances, lhs, rhs, delta, tolerance (190-205 and 237-251)
- **Side effects:** none - no logging, no I/O, no state (the module docstring's 'What This Module Does NOT Do' at 38-44 matches the code)
- **Unknowns:** The signature/behaviour of CapitalInvariantViolation (core.exceptions is not in this batch) - whether it accepts these kwargs as a context dict is asserted by the docstring (INV4) but not visible here.; Who calls assert_capital_invariant and with which bucket values (fund_manager is not in this batch).

#### `capital.kill_switch` - `capital/kill_switch.py`

**Role:** kill-switch gate + emergency flatten orchestrator | **Lines:** 2,151 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Implements the system-wide trading halt used as the last-mile gate before any order: three states (INACTIVE / SOFT_KILL / HARD_KILL, :175-179) held in memory behind an RLock (:284) and persisted to a single kill_switch_state row (:966) persist-first so a failed write aborts the state change. Its importers (main, orders.order_placer, orders.order_reconciler, orders.eod_squareoff, capital.fund_manager, capital.drift_handler, scripts.clear_kill_switch, scripts.system_manager) call is_active(intent) (:510) to block entries/exits, soft_kill()/hard_kill() to trip it, and clear_stale_state()/auto_clear_scheduled_kill() at boot (:315, :366) to clear yesterday's or a scheduled kill. On HARD_KILL with an adapter injected it also PERFORMS the flatten itself: it dispatches a non-daemon worker thread (:1193) that runs _exit_all_trades_indestructible (:1670) -- cancel resting SL/TGT, cancel CO brackets, place marketable-LIMIT exits, sweep orphan broker positions, retry with backoff until a 2h deadline.

- **Imported by (38):** `capital.drift_handler`, `capital.fund_manager`, `main`, `orders.eod_squareoff`, `orders.order_placer`, `orders.order_reconciler`, `scripts.clear_kill_switch`, `scripts.system_manager`, `tests.crash_test.ct_day3_isolated`, `tests.crash_test.ct_day3_isolated_batch2` ... (+28)
- **Imports locally (9):** `alerts.delivery`, `broker.auth_recovery`, `broker.position_helpers`, `core.constants`, `core.effect_telemetry`, `core.events`, `core.exceptions`, `core.state_store`, `core.time_authority`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`

#### `capital.position_sizer` - `capital/position_sizer.py`

**Role:** sizing | **Lines:** 877 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Turns a signal (symbol, side, entry price, SL price, intent, score tier) into an integer share quantity, by taking min(qty_by_risk, qty_by_capital, qty_by_concentration), applying a tier x performance multiplier (or a flat-rupee ceiling), rounding to lot size and then running a tail of rejection guards (SL distance, qty explosion, lot skew, position-value cap, minimum). Reads capital only through FundManager.get_snapshot() (line 375) and never reserves anything. Used by capital.risk_engine, main and eight test modules.

#### `capital.risk_engine` - `capital/risk_engine.py`

**Role:** pre-trade-gate | **Lines:** 839 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pre-trade approval gate: given a SizingResult it runs ten portfolio checks in a fixed order (KILL_SWITCH, SIZING_VALID, CAPITAL, OPEN_POSITIONS, DAILY_TRADES, CONSECUTIVE_LOSSES, DAILY_LOSS, SECTOR_EXPOSURE, CONTRARY_POSITION, DUPLICATE_SYMBOL) and short-circuits on the first failure, returning a frozen ApprovalResult. It reads FundManager and StateStore but never mutates state. Per-book limits are resolved once (327-343): a positional (delivery) entry is gated on the delivery-scoped limits and an absent one raises. Used by main plus eight test modules.

(+6)

#### `capital.strategy_governor` - `capital/strategy_governor.py`

**Role:** strategy-circuit-breaker | **Lines:** 196 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Per-strategy intraday circuit breaker: on each signal, check(strategy_name, now_time) compares the strategy's realized P&L for today against loss_multiplier x its average losing-day P&L over a lookback window, and pauses the strategy for the rest of the process's life when today's loss is worse. The pause set is in-memory only (line 42). Imported by main, a crash test and one unit test.

- **Imported by (3):** `main`, `tests.crash_test.ct_day3_isolated_batch2`, `tests.unit.test_fix130_strategy_governor`

#### `orders.closure_classifier` - `orders/closure_classifier.py`

**Role:** pure-decision | **Lines:** 162 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

CHECK1's evidence classifier for the reconciler (orders.order_reconciler). Given an Evidence bundle about a trade whose broker position vanished, it returns a Verdict naming the closure_source and the severity. It is PURE — no DB, broker, clock or logging (docstring lines 3-5) — so the decision that previously mis-marked 35 of 41 CLOSED_MANUAL trades (docstring lines 15-18) can be exhaustively tested. Its rule: suppress the CRITICAL only on positive evidence that one of OUR legs closed the position; every unknown resolves to EXTERNAL_UNATTRIBUTED at CRITICAL.

- **Imported by (2):** `orders.order_reconciler`, `tests.unit.test_closure_classifier`

#### `orders.cnc_gtt` - `orders/cnc_gtt.py`

**Role:** gtt-placer | **Lines:** 262 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Places and maintains the single broker-side two-leg OCO GTT that protects a CNC (delivery) position overnight, once its entry fills — because day-validity SL/TGT legs expire at EOD and would leave the position naked (docstring lines 4-7). Called by orders.full_entry_engine.place_deferred_exits and constructed in main; one GTT per trade, held in an in-memory map that is mirrored to the durable gtt_state table.

#### `orders.cnc_gtt_monitor` - `orders/cnc_gtt_monitor.py`

**Role:** reconciler | **Lines:** 753 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The shared reconcile routine (startup and the 15-minute in-hours cadence) that keeps each delivery position's ONE broker-side OCO GTT in sync with reality, treating the broker GTT as authority and gtt_state as the durable mirror. Per ACTIVE row it applies the K6 ladder (healthy / GTT_EXIT finalisation / F6 re-protect / auto-recreate or pre-open queue / qty-mismatch CRITICAL / orphan delete / soft-kill on >1 ACTIVE GTT), and it also carries the FIX-183 adoption prepass that order_reconciler runs before CHECK1. Imported by main.

#### `orders.eod_squareoff` - `orders/eod_squareoff.py`

**Role:** eod-squareoff-orchestrator | **Lines:** 1,887 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Implements the 15:17 IST end-of-day square-off sequence for this system: it cancels pending intraday entry orders and live SL/TGT exit legs, then exits every open MIS/CO position (CO by cancelling the entry bracket, MIS by MARKET or aggressive-LIMIT-then-MARKET), writes one eod_squareoff_log row per fire, publishes EodSquareoffComplete, resets fund_manager daily PnL and sends the Telegram DAILY SUMMARY. It is driven by an external poller (main.py calls check_and_fire(now)); it deliberately never touches CNC/delivery positions and never waits for fill confirmations, handing fills to order_monitor. It also owns restart recovery: on post_wire_init() it inspects today's log row and may fire late (IN_PROGRESS crash, or no row while past EOD but before market close).

- **Imported by (9):** `main`, `tests.integration.test_end_to_end_smoke`, `tests.unit.test_alert_direction_eod_29jun`, `tests.unit.test_e4_event_loop_safety`, `tests.unit.test_eod_squareoff`, `tests.unit.test_fix063_two_pass_eod`, `tests.unit.test_kill_switch_product_filter`, `tests.unit.test_paper_product_fidelity`, `tests.unit.test_strategy_direction_routing`
- **Imports locally (17):** `broker.order_monitor`, `broker.order_state_machine`, `broker.position_helpers`, `broker.zerodha_adapter`, `capital.fund_manager`, `capital.kill_switch`, `core.constants`, `core.effect_telemetry`, `core.events`, `core.exceptions`, `core.ids`, `core.logger` ... (+5)
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `EodFireResult` (L78) - frozen dataclass summary of one fire: date, timestamp, position/cancel attempted-succeeded-failed counts, duration_sec, recovery_fire flag. | `EodSquareoff` (L96) - the module's only class; constructor (line 111) takes adapter, state_store, fund_manager, state_machine, bus, market_windows, time_authority, kill_switch, logger and optional order_monitor/notifier/entry_gate plus exit-protocol tuning. | `EodSquareoff.post_wire_init` (L182) - H-7 deferred init: runs _check_restart_recovery() only after the caller has wired bus subscribers, because recovery can publish EodSquareoffComplete. | `EodSquareoff.check_and_fire` (L202) - the polled entry point; returns False unless market_windows.is_eod_squareoff_due(now) and not a holiday, claims the per-date flag under a lock (line 227) and calls _fire, resetting the flag if _fire raises. | `EodSquareoff.fire_now` (L238) - manual/emergency fire that bypasses the time and holiday checks, logs CRITICAL, and releases the per-date claim again if _fire raised or left positions_failed/cancels_failed > 0 (line 285). | `EodSquareoff.start_polling` (L290) - optional daemon thread named eod_squareoff_poll that calls check_and_fire(now_ist()) every poll_interval_sec, swallowing per-iteration exceptions via log_exception (line 304). | `EodSquareoff.is_alive` (L314) - read-only /health probe: True only if the start_polling thread exists and is alive; False in external-scheduler mode. | `EodSquareoff._fire` (L326) - the whole sequence: gate clear, retest clear, write-ahead log row, soft_kill, Pass-1 cancels, 2s sleep (line 424), Pass-2 exits, stale-order sweep, log COMPLETE, WAL checkpoint, publish, reset_daily_pnl, resume, Telegram summary, telemetry inc (line 569). | `EodSquareoff._cancel_pending_entries` (L864) - cancels every row from get_pending_intraday_orders, sets trades+orders status CANCELLED and releases the fund_manager reservation; returns (attempted, succeeded, failed). | `EodSquareoff._exit_open_positions` (L1027) - Pass-2 core: filters DB rows against broker get_positions (line 1104), takes broker qty as authoritative (line 1260), places CO-bracket cancels or MARKET/LIMIT exits, tracks them, records EOD order rows, then promotes unfilled LIMITs and sweeps residuals. | `EodSquareoff._sweep_residual_broker_positions` (L1427) - FIX-182 sweep: re-queries broker positions and flattens any non-zero MIS/CO position not handled above, but only if a local trade exists for that symbol today; #### `orders.full_entry_engine` - `orders/full_entry_engine.py`

**Role:** router | **Lines:** 236 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The single-shot entry engine (G10 default) that main and orders.order_placer construct: a thin router from an order_protocol string to either CoPlusTgtProtocol or LimitTripleProtocol, with no business logic of its own. #### `orders.mis_autosquareoff` - `orders/mis_autosquareoff.py`

**Role:** scheduled-safety-orchestrator | **Lines:** 1,324 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The PRIMARY MIS safety orchestrator added alongside (never replacing) the 15:15 SOFT_KILL and the 15:17 EOD routine: one daemon poll thread runs two passes — PASS_1 at cutoff-first_offset and PASS_2 at cutoff-second_offset — that find open equity-MIS broker positions, cancel that symbol's resting MIS SL/TGT legs, verify the cancel at the broker, then submit a protected MARKET exit before Zerodha's earliest CAS auto-square-off. It carries the 03/04-Sep incident fixes: F1 restores the stop on every failure path, F2 polls the cancel to a bounded deadline, F1b verifies that a restored stop is actually resting, and Block A blocks PASS_2 while a PASS_1 order is unresolved. Imported by main.

- **Imported by (5):** `main`, `tests.unit.test_mis_autosquareoff`, `tests.unit.test_mis_market_protection`, `tests.unit.test_mis_squareoff_notifier`, `tests.unit.test_mis_squareoff_restore_and_settle`
- **Imports locally (2):** `core.ids`, `core.mis_squareoff_timing`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `MisState` (L152) - the outcome vocabulary every terminal path names (NO_MIS ... PROTECTION_UNKNOWN) | `SymbolOutcome` (L212) - per-symbol record incl. the submitted market_protection percent, pass_1_order_id and residual_detail | `PassResult` (L232) - per-pass record: lateness, query timestamps, remaining budget, deadline_preserved, symbols, notes | `MisAutoSquareoff` (L248) - the orchestrator; adapter/store/logger/timing/now_fn/holiday_fn all injected, no implicit clock | `check_and_fire` (L320) - evaluates both passes under one lock; PASS_2 takes priority once now>=CHECK_2 (B-2) and PASS_1 retries are bounded | `start_polling` (L389) - starts ONE daemon thread calling check_and_fire synchronously so the passes can never overlap | `is_alive` (L422) - read-only liveness of the poll thread for /health | `_run_pass` (L431) - fresh get_positions (a failure is BROKER_STATE_UNAVAILABLE, never flat), scan log, pre-flight budget check that never skips, execute, verify | `_execute_mis_auto_squareoff` (L702) - per symbol: Block A residual gate (PASS_2) -> cancel resting legs -> verify -> submit the protected MARKET exit; restores the stop on every failure | `_restore_protection` (L917) - F1 undo: re-places the SL leg with its original parameters; never raises (wraps _restore_protection_inner at 945) | `_verify_restored` (L1133) - F1b: polls the broker order history to a 5s budget; CONFIRMED / FILLED / FAILED / PROTECTION_UNKNOWN | `_verify_cancelled` (L1177) - F2: polls each cancelled order to a single shared 5s deadline; False means a settled negative

#### `orders.order_manager` - `orders/order_manager.py`

**Role:** persistence | **Lines:** 769 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The thin DB layer for the orders/ layer: it owns every INSERT/UPDATE against the `trades` and `orders` tables and does no arithmetic — callers (order_placer, order_reconciler, main) supply every value. It assigns trade_id, records entry fills and best-effort latency metrics, and is the single central finalizer close_trade() that writes the exit columns, the cost breakdown and the W8 closure axes under a compare-and-swap WHERE. Optionally subscribes to OrderStatusChanged to persist broker status snapshots.

#### `orders.order_placer` - `orders/order_placer.py`

**Role:** entry-order-orchestrator | **Lines:** 4,706 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Single orchestrator for one trade's whole order lifecycle: place() (line 871) creates the trades row, runs the last-mile gates (kill-switch, EOD cutoff, entry-slippage, margin top-up, liquidity), calls FullEntryEngine.execute (1345) for the ENTRY leg only, persists the order rows and registers each leg with OrderMonitor plus an in-memory _fill_map. It then handles the resulting events it subscribed to at construction (693, 697, 700): on ENTRY fill it commits the capital reservation, records the fill and places the deferred SL/TGT at the ACTUAL filled qty (2156-2171); on SL/TGT/EOD fill it closes the trade, cancels the OCO sibling and releases used capital (2275-2526). Its callers are main.py (wiring), signals.signal_processor (the only production caller of place()), core.config_auditor (imports the pure validator at 279) and the unit/integration tests listed in the manifest.

#### `orders.order_protocol_co` - `orders/order_protocol_co.py`

**Role:** broker-protocol | **Lines:** 255 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The CO_PLUS_TGT entry protocol (P8/P13 intraday default): phase 1 places the Cover Order entry at the broker with variety='co', order_type='SL' and trigger_price=sl_price so Zerodha maintains the SL bracket; phase 2, called after the entry fills, places the separate LIMIT TGT on the closing side. Used by main, FullEntryEngine and the order_placer tests. Its effect-telemetry handle is a dormancy tripwire — the comment at line 86 records that CO has never actually been placed in production (805/805 regular).

- **Imported by (10):** `main`, `orders.full_entry_engine`, `tests.integration.conftest`, `tests.integration.test_emergency_exit_chain`, `tests.unit.test_a3_entry_kill_recheck`, `tests.unit.test_e3_hygiene`, `tests.unit.test_fix129_rejection_handling`, `tests.unit.test_order_placer`, `tests.unit.test_slice1_rr_aftercheck`, `tests.unit.test_tgt_retry`
- **Imports locally (7):** `broker.zerodha_adapter`, `core.effect_telemetry`, `core.exceptions`, `core.ids`, `core.logger`, `orders.entry_engine`, `orders.full_entry_engine`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `_exit_side` (L61) - BUY -> SELL, anything else -> BUY | `CoPlusTgtProtocol` (L69) - the two-phase CO protocol; holds the adapter, logger and the effect handle | `CoPlusTgtProtocol.__init__` (L78) - takes the ZerodhaAdapter and logger; registers the 'co_protocol' effect handle (line 87) | `execute` (L91) - phase 1: truncates the tag, places the CO entry, rejects an empty broker_order_id, returns EntryResult(success=True) with empty SL/TGT fields | `place_exits` (L169) - phase 2: places the LIMIT TGT on the exit side; CRITICALs and raises on failure or an empty id
- **Inputs:** execute(symbol, side, qty, entry_price, sl_price, tgt_price, intent, trade_id, tag, entry_order_type) (lines 91-104); place_exits(symbol, entry_side, qty, tgt_price, intent, trade_id, tag) (lines 169-178)
- **Outputs:** EntryResult with order_protocol='CO_PLUS_TGT', sl_broker_order_id='' (SL lives in the CO bracket) and tgt fields empty (lines 156-165); ExitLegsResult with the TGT ids and price, sl_* zeroed (lines 246-255); logs: DEBUG co_plus_tgt.order_tag_truncated (116), INFO co_plus_tgt.co_placed phase1_only (144), CRITICAL co_plus_tgt.tgt_failed_phase2 (211) and tgt_empty_broker_id_phase2 (224), INFO tgt_placed phase2_complete (237)
- **Side effects:** two broker order placements (lines 123, 200); effect-telemetry increment on every execute (line 113); raises OrderRejectedError on an empty broker_order_id (139, 232) and re-raises BrokerError (135, 220); no DB writes (docstring line 41)
- **External calls:** adapter.place_order(variety='co', order_type='SL', trigger_price=sl_price) — the CO entry (line 123); adapter.place_order(order_type='LIMIT') — the TGT leg (line 200)
- **Log calls by level:** debug 1 | info 2 | critical 2
- **Unknowns:** whether ExitLegsResult's sl_order_type='' / sl_trigger_price=0.0 (249-250) are meaningful to the caller — the dataclass is defined in orders.order_protocol_limit and imported indirectly at line 193; what truncate_tag_for_broker does to a 32-hex trade_id beyond the 16-char limit stated at line 114; whether the CO variety is reachable at all in production (the comment at line 86 says CO is declared by 12/15 YAMLs and discarded elsewhere)

#### `orders.order_protocol_limit` - `orders/order_protocol_limit.py`

**Role:** order-protocol | **Lines:** 622 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Implements the LIMIT_TRIPLE order protocol as a two-phase sequence for OrderPlacer/full_entry_engine: execute() places only the ENTRY order, and place_exits() later places the SL (always order_type="SL" stop-limit, line 374) plus TGT LIMIT sized to the ACTUAL filled qty. It also offers place_tgt_only() so TGTRetryManager can re-attempt a target on a trade whose SL is already standing. All three paths first clamp/validate exit prices against the instrument's circuit band via clamp_exit_into_band and refuse to send a wrong-side exit.

(+5)

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`

#### `orders.order_reconciler` - `orders/order_reconciler.py`

**Role:** reconciliation-daemon | **Lines:** 4,621 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Runs a 15-second daemon poll (line 446) that compares local trade/order state in SQLite against live Zerodha state and repairs the difference: six position/order checks (docstring RC5, lines 19-26), a crash-recovery SL placer (G5b, line 3159), a duplicate-exit-leg canceller (line 3472), two capital-drift comparisons (line 3693, line 3994), a CO stop-trigger drift observer (line 4081), and an in-flight-entry recovery prepass that correlates a timed-out or crashed ENTRY back to the broker by tag and adopts or fails it (line 4169). It is constructed and started by main and is the only module in its import set that both places broker orders and releases capital on the reconciliation path; the rest of its importers are the ten unit/integration tests named in the manifest.

(+10)

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`

non-COSMETIC ones are persisted | `correlate_entry_by_tag` (L217) - pure function returning ("MATCH"|"ABSENT"|"AMBIGUOUS", order) by matching truncate_tag_for_broker(trade_id) plus entry side against broker orders | `OrderReconciler` (L264) - the class; #### `orders.price_math` - `orders/price_math.py`

**Role:** pure-calculation | **Lines:** 377 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Pure, dependency-free arithmetic module that is the single source of truth for tick rounding and every SL/TGT/exit price formula used across the order stack (imported by 10 modules including zerodha_adapter, order_placer, cnc_gtt, eod_squareoff, shadow_tracker). It also owns the shared default constants (tick 0.05, emergency buffer 1%, SL limit offset 0.5%, circuit margin 2%) and the circuit-band clamp that decides whether an exit price is placeable at all.

- **Imported by (22):** `broker.zerodha_adapter`, `orders.breakeven_manager`, `orders.cnc_gtt`, `orders.eod_squareoff`, `orders.order_placer`, `orders.order_protocol_limit`, `orders.order_reconciler`, `orders.shadow_tracker`, `orders.sl_breach_monitor`, `orders.structure_exit_manager` ... (+12)
- **Imports locally (0):** none
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `DEFAULT_TICK` (L27) - fallback NSE equity tick 0.05 used when no InstrumentCache tick is supplied | `round_to_tick` (L30) - Decimal-based tick snap with mode "up"/"down"/"nearest"; tick<=0 falls back to round(price,2) | `tier_slippage_tolerance_rs` (L60) - picks the max acceptable entry slippage in Rs from (max_price, max_slip) bands, first band with price < max_price wins | `_is_long` (L79) - maps "LONG"/"BUY" to True, everything else to False | `calc_sl_price` (L84) - FIXED_PCT stop: entry*(1-sl_pct) for long, entry*(1+sl_pct) for short | `calc_tgt_price` (L100) - RISK_REWARD target: entry ± abs(entry-sl)*rr_ratio | `EMERGENCY_EXIT_BUFFER_PCT` (L124) - 1% default buffer through the touch for a marketable-limit emergency exit | `marketable_limit_price` (L127) - LTP ± buffer, rounded down for SELL / up for BUY; raises ValueError on ltp<=0 or a bad side | `DEFAULT_SL_LIMIT_OFFSET_PCT` (L159) - 0.5% default offset of an SL order's limit past its trigger | `calc_sl_limit_price` (L162) - stop-limit limit price = trigger*(1∓offset), directionally tick-snapped so it stays past the trigger | `calc_gtt_limit_price` (L220) - same math for one CNC OCO-GTT leg, but the offset must be passed explicitly (no intraday default inherited) | `clamp_exit_into_band` (L284) - clamps a price into [lower*(1+margin), upper*(1-margin)] then checks leg/direction polarity against the entry fill, returning ClampResult(placeable=...)
- **Inputs:** prices, ticks and percentage fractions as plain floats (all functions); tiers as a list of (max_price, max_slippage_rs) tuples (60-64); clamp_exit_into_band(price, leg="TGT"|"SL", direction, entry_fill, upper_circuit, lower_circuit, tick, margin_pct) (284-294)
- **Outputs:** floats (rounded/derived prices) from every calc_* and round_to_tick; ClampResult(price, was_clamped, placeable, reason) frozen dataclass (259-281); ValueError for a bad side or non-positive trigger/ltp (146, 153, 202, 206, 215, 245, 247, 252) and for leg not in (TGT, SL) (332)
- **Paper vs live:** none visible — the module is pure arithmetic with no adapter, mode flag or I/O (docstring states "PURE: no quote I/O" at 322).
- **Unknowns:** Which callers pass a real InstrumentCache tick versus falling through to DEFAULT_TICK — not determinable here.; Whether capital.emergency_exit_buffer_pct / capital.sl_limit_offset_pct actually override the module defaults at runtime; the docstrings assert it (123, 157) but no read happens in this file.; The real circuit-margin used in production: DEFAULT_CIRCUIT_MARGIN_PCT (256) is only a default parameter value.

#### `orders.shadow_tracker` - `orders/shadow_tracker.py`

**Role:** post-trade-simulation | **Lines:** 896 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

After a real trade closes, records "inning 1" (the real trade) and then price-simulates up to max_innings further innings for the same symbol with no broker orders, closing each on a simulated SL/TGT hit and cascading to the next. It is driven by PositionClosed / EodSquareoffComplete on the EventBus plus on_tick() from the live feed, and exposes is_tracking(symbol) so signal_processor can avoid re-entering a symbol still being simulated.

- **Imported by (8):** `main`, `tests.integration.test_hardening_scenarios`, `tests.unit.test_e4_event_loop_safety`, `tests.unit.test_fix142_final_cleanup`, `tests.unit.test_shadow_tracker`, `tests.unit.test_state_store`, `tests.unit.test_time_authority_sweep`, `tests.unit.test_zerodha_adapter`
- **Imports locally (6):** `core.constants`, `core.effect_telemetry`, `core.events`, `core.logger`, `core.time_authority`, `orders.price_math`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `Inning` (L65) - frozen 15-field snapshot of one inning; is_real True only for inning 1 | `ShadowTracker` (L94) - the tracker; subscribes to PositionClosed and EodSquareoffComplete at construction (194-195) | `ShadowTracker.__init__` (L119) - injects store/bus/live_feed/market_windows/time_authority/notifier/strategies, restores _eod_fired from eod_squareoff_log, subscribes handlers | `set_instrument_cache` (L199) - wires the token→symbol cache that on_tick needs | `is_tracking` (L203) - True if any active simulated inning holds that symbol (returns False when disabled) | `on_tick` (L221) - resolves the tick's symbol, records last price, and closes any matching inning whose SL/TGT is hit | `_on_position_closed` (L269) - builds inning 1 from the trades row (recalculating a RISK_REWARD TGT from the actual fill), persists it, alerts, then cascades | `_on_eod_complete` (L415) - date-idempotent EOD handler: marks _eod_fired, closes every active inning at last price, then clears the map | `_start_simulated_inning` (L465) - creates inning N+1 at the previous inning's exit price with derived SL/TGT, persists it and registers it active | `_close_inning` (L534) - computes PnL, calls update_inning_close, pops from the active map only on DB success, alerts, then cascades | `_check_hit` (L768) - module-level SL/TGT hit test; uses bid (LONG) / ask (SHORT) for the stop when available, else LTP; SL is tested before TGT | `_derive_sl_tgt_from_strategy` (L813) - applies a strategy's sl_method/tgt_method (ATR falls back to FIXED_PCT) with sl_min_pct/sl_max_pct bounds
- **Inputs:** PositionClosed events (trade_id, exit_price) (269-277); EodSquareoffComplete events (415); tick dicts with instrument_token, last_price, optional bid/ask (221-249); constructor collaborators: state_store, bus, live_feed, market_windows, time_authority, notifier, logger, strategies dict, max_innings, alert_per_inning, enabled, mode (119-133); trades rows: direction, entry_actual_price, entry_target_price, sl_initial, tgt_initial, strategy, exit_reason, entry_time, created_at, exit_time, qty_filled (281-358, 611-613, 706)

#### `orders.slippage_recorder` - `orders/slippage_recorder.py`

**Role:** telemetry-persistence | **Lines:** 299 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Write-only execution-quality recorder: it subscribes asynchronously to OrderFilled and PositionClosed and writes three raw-fact rows — one order_execution_log row per fill, a best-effort market_execution_context row (bid/ask/spread at fill), and one trade_slippage_log roll-up per closed trade carrying rr_damage_pct. It computes no analytics and is written so that any failure loses a log row, never a trade (every handler is try/except-wrapped).

#### `orders.smart_tgt_manager` - `orders/smart_tgt_manager.py`

**Role:** sl-trailing | **Lines:** 896 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Trails the stop on Cover-Order trades by modifying the CO trigger_price in place (never cancel-replace), driven by CandleStore 1-minute close callbacks rather than its own thread. It keeps per-trade state (best_price, current_sl, trail_count, consecutive_failures) in memory and mirrored in the smart_tgt_state table so a restart can rehydrate, and it only updates local state after the broker confirms the modify (the "ghost SL" rule).

- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`, `concurrent`

#### `orders.structure_exit_manager` - `orders/structure_exit_manager.py`

**Role:** exit-controller | **Lines:** 672 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Structure-aware exit controller for MIS LIMIT_TRIPLE trades only, subscribed to CandleStore 1-minute closes. On each close for a held symbol it either exits the position outright when the candle CLOSE breaks the nearest HIGH-confidence S/R band (mark trades.status=EXITING, cancel the resting SL+TGT, then flatten with a marketable LIMIT or MARKET), or else trails the static SL leg to the structure band via adapter.modify_order, tighten-only and at most once per candle.

- **Imported by (2):** `main`, `tests.unit.test_structure_exit_manager`
- **Imports locally (4):** `core.ids`, `core.time_authority`, `orders.price_math`, `sr_detector.retest_confirm`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `StructureExitManager` (L82) - the controller; dormant unless config.structure_exit_enabled (127-129) | `StructureExitManager.__init__` (L97) - keyword-only wiring of adapter, state_store, zone_cache, config, logger, now_fn, instrument_cache, order_monitor, candle_store, notifier, mode, sl_limit_offset_pct, enabled | `start` (L144) - subscribes on_1m_close to candle_store and sets _running; no-op when disabled | `stop` (L163) - unsubscribes (best-effort) and clears _running/_subscribed | `on_1m_close` (L175) - the candle callback; never raises (catches and logs so sibling callbacks survive) | `_process_close` (L187) - finds the open trade, resolves direction, reads zones, selects the zone, then ACTION B (break) before ACTION A (trail) | `_find_open_limit_triple_trade` (L229) - filters get_open_intraday_positions() to symbol + order_protocol == LIMIT_TRIPLE; warns and uses the first if several | `_select_zone` (L256) - nearest min-confidence support below (LONG) / resistance above (SHORT) the candle extreme | `_is_confirmed_break` (L281) - 1m close beyond the band (± break_buffer_pct) plus the polarity-flipped _strong_close when require_strong_close | `_exit_on_break` (L300) - SE5 sequence: _mark_exiting, then cancel resting exits, then flatten, then a best-effort Telegram WARNING | `_mark_exiting` (L349) - UPDATE trades SET status='EXITING'; returns False on failure so the exit aborts for this candle | `_flatten_one` (L412) - reverse-aware flatten: skips when broker is flat, prefers broker qty, marketable LIMIT with MARKET fallback, tracks via order_monitor and inserts an EOD orders leg
- **Inputs:** CandleData objects (symbol, high, low, close, ts) from candle_store (187-218, 517); config attributes (127-134); ZoneSet from zone_cache.get(symbol) with .support/.resistance zones carrying band_low, band_high, confidence (204, 264-277); open trade rows: symbol, order_protocol, direction, trade_id, qty_filled (242-245, 303-304, 428); orders rows: order_id, leg, trigger_price, price, status (369-372, 606-611); broker positions (symbol, product, qty) and quotes (last_price) (625-644)

#### `orders.tgt_retry_manager` - `orders/tgt_retry_manager.py`

**Role:** retry-daemon | **Lines:** 394 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Background daemon that closes the FIX-190 Bug C gap: for trades flagged needs_tgt_retry (SL standing, TGT never placed), it wakes every poll_interval_sec, picks the candidates whose exponential-backoff window has elapsed, and calls order_placer.retry_tgt_for_trade(trade_id), then clears or bumps the flag and sends Telegram INFO/WARNING. It also self-detects its own crash loop and exposes health_snapshot() for the /health endpoint.

- **Imported by (3):** `main`, `tests.unit.test_nocil_clamp_fix`, `tests.unit.test_tgt_retry`
- **Imports locally (3):** `core.effect_telemetry`, `core.market_windows`, `core.time_authority`
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `TGTRetryManager` (L50) - the daemon class | `TGTRetryManager.__init__` (L53) - keyword-only wiring of state_store, order_placer, notifier, kill_switch, logger, poll_interval_sec, max_attempts, backoff_base_sec, enabled, mode, market_hours_guard, crash-alert thresholds | `start` (L102) - spawns the daemon thread 'tgt-retry-manager'; logs and returns when disabled | `stop` (L122) - sets the stop event and joins the thread with a 5s timeout | `_loop` (L129) - runs run_once immediately then every poll interval; records each cycle as success or failure and never dies | `_record_cycle_success` (L142) - resets the failure counter and, after a crash loop, logs + sends a recovery INFO | `_record_cycle_failure` (L164) - counts a raised cycle and fires one throttled crash alert past the threshold | `health_snapshot` (L216) - returns ok/state (disabled|dead|crash_loop|running), consecutive_failures and last clean cycle | `_backoff_for` (L244) - base * 2**retry_count seconds before the next attempt | `_is_due` (L248) - True when the backoff has elapsed; a missing/unparseable timestamp counts as due | `run_once` (L260) - non-reentrant entry point (returns [] if a cycle is already running); returns the per-trade outcome strings | `_run_once_locked` (L269) - kill-switch and market-hours guards, then per-candidate dispatch to order_placer.retry_tgt_for_trade
- **Inputs:** constructor: state_store, order_placer, notifier, kill_switch, logger, poll_interval_sec (30), max_attempts (5), backoff_base_sec (30), enabled, mode, market_hours_guard, crash_alert_threshold (3), crash_realert_interval_sec (3600) (53-69); candidate rows from state_store.get_tgt_retry_candidates(): trade_id, symbol, tgt_retry_count, tgt_last_retry_at (293, 302-305); kill_switch.is_active() (275); current IST time via now_ist() (282); outcome strings returned by order_placer.retry_tgt_for_trade (313)
- **Outputs:** a list of outcome strings from run_once (260, 324); flag mutations via clear_needs_tgt_retry (330, 348, 358) and bump_tgt_retry (356); Telegram INFO on a placed retry (336), WARNING on give-up (366), CRITICAL on crash loop (204), INFO on recovery (151); health_snapshot() dict (232-240)
- **Side effects:** starts a daemon thread (109-112); DB reads/writes through state_store helpers: get_tgt_retry_candidates (293), clear_needs_tgt_retry (330, 348, 358), bump_tgt_retry (356); indirect broker order placement through order_placer.retry_tgt_for_trade (313); Telegram sends via the notifier (389); effect-telemetry counter _fx_retry.inc() before each retry dispatch (312); mutates crash-loop counters and timestamps (160-162, 168, 195)

#### `reports.daily_report` - `reports/daily_report.py`

**Role:** scheduled-entry-point / xlsx-report-builder | **Lines:** 1,946 | **Scheduled entry point:** yes

Builds a 7-sheet .xlsx EOD report for one trading date by pulling that date's rows out of StateStore and writing openpyxl worksheets (0_EOD_Dashboard, 1_Signals, 2_Orders, 3_Capital, 4_Candles, 5_Telegram, 6_Strategy_Analysis); the file lands at <output-dir>/daily_report_<date>.xlsx (line 1810). It is a standalone cron/CLI entry point (module docstring lines 7-13 show `python -m reports.daily_report` and a `5 16 * * 1-5` crontab line), refuses to run on weekends/NSE holidays unless --force (line 1882), and records a cron heartbeat for the Control Tower on both success and failure (lines 1911, 1927). Its only importers in the hint are tests; nothing else in the tree imports it.

#### `reports.daily_trade_review` - `reports/daily_trade_review.py`

**Role:** scheduled-report-entry-point | **Lines:** 2,924 | **Scheduled entry point:** yes

Builds one consolidated multi-sheet Excel workbook per trading date from the SQLite state store alone, and writes it to reports/output/daily_trade_review_report_<date>.xlsx (line 2835). It renders ten sheets in a fixed order -- Dashboard, Reconciliation, Orders, Signals, Strategies, Slippage, Capital, Candles, Telegram, Config (lines 2824-2833) -- where Orders is one row per trade with 68 columns in 9 colour-banded groups (_COLSPECS, line 141), Reconciliation is four PASS/FAIL/PENDING_CAPTURE identity blocks (lines 1015, 1033, 1071, 1078), and the Dashboard re-derives its headlines from the same already-built record lists so it cannot disagree with the detail sheets (lines 2070-2074). It is invoked as a cron/CLI entry point via main() (line 2865), which self-guards on weekends and NSE holidays (line 2889) and records a cron heartbeat either way (lines 2897, 2911, 2915); its only in-repo importers are four unit tests.

#### `reports.signal_status` - `reports/signal_status.py`

**Role:** status-classification (pure) | **Lines:** 156 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

The single place where a `signals.status` string is classified, for both Excel report builders (`reports.daily_report`, `reports.daily_trade_review`). It exists to forbid branching on the free-text `rejection_reason`: the module docstring records that counting rejections by substring reported 1,189 CAPITAL rejections when the true count was 0 (docstring lines 13-17) and that grouping by the free text produced 190 breakdown lines instead of 9 (lines 18-20). Pure string functions, no I/O.

- **Imported by (3):** `reports.daily_report`, `reports.daily_trade_review`, `tests.unit.test_signal_status_classification`
- **Imports locally (0):** none
- **Third-party / external:** `__future__`
- **Key symbols:** `QUALIFIED_STATUSES` (L40) - set of statuses meaning the signal reached an order attempt (PROCESSED/TRADED/PLACEMENT_FAILED/RESERVED/PROCESSED_NO_PLACER/PASSED) | `_SCORE_STATUS_RE` (L53) - regex `^REJECTED_SCORE_\d+$` used to collapse the per-score rejection family | `KNOWN_OTHER_STATUSES` (L46) - set of known non-terminal/other statuses; anything outside all sets becomes 'unmapped' | `SIZING_PREFIX` (L57) - the literal prefix 'REJECTED_SIZING_' for the position_sizer family | `_RISK_ENGINE_SIZING_STATUS` (L65) - 'REJECTED_SIZING_VALID', excluded from the sizing family because it is the risk engine's check-2 name, not a sizer constraint | `bucket` (L68) - maps a status to qualified/rejected/skipped/dropped/other/unmapped by prefix and set membership | `is_rejected` (L85) - True only for the REJECTED_* family (bucket == 'rejected') | `has_explicit_disposition` (L96) - True when the status is known at all, i.e. non-blank and bucket != 'unmapped' | `is_dedup_duplicate` (L115) - exact equality with 'DUPLICATE' (deliberately not a substring test) | `is_sizing_rejection` (L125) - True for REJECTED_SIZING_* except REJECTED_SIZING_VALID | `sizing_constraint` (L136) - returns the constraint name after the REJECTED_SIZING_ prefix, else None | `family` (L147) - identity for every status except REJECTED_SCORE_<n>, which collapses to 'REJECTED_SCORE'
- **Inputs:** a single Optional[str] status per function (arguments at lines 68, 85, 96, 115, 125, 136, 147)
- **Outputs:** bucket label str (line 82); bool verdicts (lines 93, 112, 122, 133); Optional[str] constraint name (line 144); grouping-family str (line 156)

#### `alerts.critical` - `alerts/critical.py`

**Role:** sentinel-file-writer | **Lines:** 199 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Writes one JSON "sentinel" file per CRITICAL alert so a separate watcher process can email it even if the trading process dies right after (docstring lines 4-8). Writes to `<sentinel_dir>/critical_alert_<YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS>_<8hex>.flag` via write-to-.tmp + fsync + os.replace (lines 116-127), and also supplies the watcher-side helpers that list, parse and rename those files. Imported by main, core.state_store, alerts.telegram_notifier and six scripts.

- **Imported by (27):** `alerts.telegram_notifier`, `core.state_store`, `main`, `scripts.alert_watcher`, `scripts.copy_gate`, `scripts.cron_officer`, `scripts.cron_watchdog`, `scripts.forward_shadow_record`, `scripts.gemini_log_review`, `scripts.liveness_probe` ... (+17)

#### `alerts.cron_alerts` - `alerts/cron_alerts.py`

**Role:** alert-policy | **Lines:** 117 | **Scheduled entry point:** no

Per-cron-job alert POLICY: maps a job's heartbeat outcome (FAILED/SUCCESS/SKIPPED/PARTIAL) plus the job's criticality to a TelegramNotifier severity and sends it, staying silent for the non-escalating combinations (lines 86-94). Called by utils.cron_heartbeat. Criticality is tri-state and an unreadable registry resolves to CRITICAL on FAILED, silent on SUCCESS (lines 77-87).

#### `alerts.mis_squareoff_notifier` - `alerts/mis_squareoff_notifier.py`

"F": human-facing delivery for the MIS auto-square-off orchestrator's four CRITICAL states (DEADLINE_BREACH, MIS_REMAINS, CANCEL_FAILED, BROKER_STATE_UNAVAILABLE — lines 65-70). It drives an email callable and a telegram callable INDEPENDENTLY, each on its own bounded worker thread, and records two separate per-channel results under one correlation id. It derives its own pre-pass trigger from the same trading-hours config as the orchestrator but never imports or shares the orchestrator's instance (lines 18-26, 164-172); main.py builds it at line 3353 and wires notify_critical as the orchestrator's critical_sink at main.py:3387.
